cam.matrix_world.inverted()\ntable = []\nfor name, point, ref in anchors:\n    clip = projection @ view @ Vector((point[0], point[1], point[2], 1))\n    row = {'name': name, 'reference': list(ref)}\n    if clip.w <= 0:\n        row['projection'] = None\n        row['status'] = 'behind camera'\n    else:\n        uv = (.5*(clip.x/clip.w+1), .5*(1-clip.y/clip.w))\n        row['projection'] = [round(v, 4) for v in uv]\n        row['residual'] = [round(uv[i]-ref[i], 4) for i in range(2)]\n    table.append(row)\nprint('ANCHOR_DIAGNOSTICS ' + json.dumps(table, separators=(',', ':')))\n"}

1))\n    if clip.w <= 0:\n        rows.append('{\"name\":\"%s\",\"reference\":[%.4f,%.4f],'\n                    '\"projection\":null,\"status\":\"behind camera\"}' %\n                    (name, ref[0], ref[1]))\n    else:\n        u = .5*(clip.x/clip.w+1)\n        v = .5*(1-clip.y/clip.w)\n        rows.append('{\"name\":\"%s\",\"reference\":[%.4f,%.4f],'\n                    '\"projection\":[%.4f,%.4f],\"residual\":[%.4f,%.4f]}' %\n                    (name, ref[0], ref[1], u, v, u-ref[0], v-ref[1]))\nprint('ANCHOR_DIAGNOSTICS [' + ','.join(rows) + ']')\n"}

cam.calc_matrix_camera(depsgraph, x=IMAGE_WIDTH, y=IMAGE_HEIGHT,\n                                        scale_x=1, scale_y=1)\n    view = cam.matrix_world.inverted()\n    result = []\n    for name, point, ref in anchors:\n        clip = projection @ view @ Vector((point[0],point[1],point[2],1))\n        if clip.w <= 0:\n            result.append((name, ref, None))\n        else:\n            result.append((name, ref, (.5*(clip.x/clip.w+1),\n                                       .5*(1-clip.y/clip.w))))\n    return result\n\n# Test small target shifts with fixed geometry.
